# Chrome-Scripts

A collection of browser bookmarklets and userscripts maintained by Cobblestone Learning.

The script catalog below is regenerated automatically by GitHub Actions on every push to `main`. Everything above the `AUTO-GENERATED:START` marker is hand-maintained and safe to edit — the workflow only rewrites the block between the two sentinel comments.

## How it works

- Drop a `.js` file in the repo root and push to `main`.
- The [Generate README](.github/workflows/generate-readme.yml) workflow asks OpenAI (`openai`, default model `gpt-4.1-nano` — 1M context, very cheap) for a description and usage notes for any new or changed script, builds a minified `javascript:` bookmarklet, and rebuilds the **Scripts** section below.
- Descriptions are cached in `.github/cache/script-descriptions.json` keyed by file content hash — unchanged scripts cost zero API calls on subsequent runs.

## Setup

Add an `OPENAI_API_KEY` repository secret (Settings → Secrets and variables → Actions). Optionally override the model with an `OPENAI_MODEL` repo variable. The workflow uses the default `GITHUB_TOKEN` to commit the regenerated README, so no PAT is needed unless branch protection rules block bot pushes to `main`.

<!-- AUTO-GENERATED:START — do not edit below this line -->
<!-- Last generated: 2026-05-26T15:32:49.322Z -->

## Scripts

### `better-sg-reports.js`

This script creates a browser overlay to display detailed SiteGround hosting reports, including server health, site usage, performance trends, and a branded PDF report generator. It is used to visualize and analyze server metrics directly from the SiteGround control panel.

**Usage**

- Open a SiteGround dashboard containing API data about server CPU, memory, and site stats.
- Run the script directly on the page; it will inject a full-featured overlay with charts and reports.
- Use the provided buttons to generate PDF reports, export CSV data, or view comparisons and trends.
- Ensure pop-ups are not blocked; the script automatically handles data collection and visualization.

**jsDelivr URL**

```
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/CobblestoneLearning/Chrome-Scripts@main/better-sg-reports.js
```

AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAP%2F%2FZ%22%2Cr%3D%7Bcpu_seconds%3A'%3Cstrong%3ECPU%20Seconds%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20total%20server%20processing%20time%20consumed%20by%20a%20site.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EEvery%20PHP%20request%2C%20database%20query%2C%20AJAX%20call%2C%20and%20cron%20job%20burns%20CPU%20seconds%20like%20fuel.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Healthy%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20consistent%20day-to-day%20values%2C%20no%20sudden%20spikes%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%20Concern%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20values%202%C3%97%20above%20the%20site%5C's%20own%207-day%20average%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Problem%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20values%205%C3%97%2B%20above%20average%2C%20or%20relentless%20escalation%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3ECommon%20spike%20causes%3A%20SCORM%2FxAPI%20state%20saves%2C%20unoptimised%20queries%2C%20bot%20traffic%2C%20broken%20cron.%3C%2Fem%3E'%2Cprogram_executions%3A'%3Cstrong%3EProgram%20Executions%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20how%20many%20times%20PHP%20was%20invoked%20on%20the%20server.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ECompare%20with%20CPU%20Seconds%20to%20understand%20root%20cause%3A%3Cbr%3E%E2%80%A2%20Exec%20%E2%86%91%20%2B%20CPU%20%E2%86%91%20together%20%E2%86%92%20%3Cem%3Evolume%20problem%3C%2Fem%3E%20(too%20many%20requests)%3Cbr%3E%E2%80%A2%20Exec%20stable%20%2B%20CPU%20%E2%86%91%20%E2%86%92%20%3Cem%3Eweight%20problem%3C%2Fem%3E%20(each%20request%20heavier)%3Cbr%3E%E2%80%A2%20Exec%20%E2%86%93%20%2B%20CPU%20%E2%86%93%20more%20%E2%86%92%20%3Cem%3Efix%20confirmed%3C%2Fem%3E%20(blocking%20wasteful%20requests%20before%20PHP%20runs)%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Good%20fix%20signal%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20CPU%2Fexec%20ratio%20drops%20%E2%80%94%20each%20request%20now%20cheaper%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Bad%20signal%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20ratio%20rises%20while%20executions%20fall%20%E2%80%94%20fewer%20requests%20but%20each%20one%20heavier'%2Ccore_pct%3A'%3Cstrong%3ECores%20in%20Use%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20how%20many%20CPU%20cores%20the%20whole%20server%20is%20using%20right%20now%20(or%20on%20a%20given%20day).%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ESiteGround%5C's%20API%20returns%20the%20raw%20value%20as%20%22%25%20of%20one%20core%22%20%E2%80%94%20100%25%20%3D%20one%20full%20core%2C%20200%25%20%3D%20two%20cores%2C%20etc.%20We%20divide%20by%20100%20to%20get%20cores.%20So%20a%20reading%20of%200.6%20cores%20on%20a%209-core%20plan%20means%20you%5C're%20using%206.7%25%20of%20plan%20capacity.%20The%20number%20is%20%3Cstrong%3Eplan-immune%3C%2Fstrong%3E%3A%200.6%20cores%20is%200.6%20cores%20whether%20the%20plan%20is%207%20or%209.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ESiteGround%20measures%20in%2015-second%20windows.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Comfortable%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20below%2050%25%20of%20plan%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%20Elevated%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%2050%E2%80%9375%25%20of%20plan%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Critical%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%2075%25%2B%20of%20plan%20%E2%80%94%20requests%20queued%2C%20users%20experience%20slowdowns%20now%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3EShort%20spikes%20are%20normal.%20Sustained%20readings%20near%20saturation%20require%20investigation.%3C%2Fem%3E'%2Ccontrol_group%3A'%3Cstrong%3EControl%20Group%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20unmodified%20sites%20used%20as%20a%20comparison%20baseline.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EIf%20all%20sites%20get%20quieter%20after%20your%20fix%2C%20that%20could%20be%20a%20quiet%20week%20%E2%80%94%20not%20your%20fix.%20The%20control%20group%20distinguishes%20fix%20effect%20from%20ambient%20traffic%20reduction.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Strong%20fix%20evidence%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20target%20drops%2C%20control%20stays%20flat%20or%20rises%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%20Ambiguous%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20both%20dropped%20but%20target%20more%20than%20control%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Weak%20evidence%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20both%20dropped%20proportionally%20%E2%80%94%20likely%20just%20a%20quiet%20period'%2Cnetwork_change%3A'%3Cstrong%3ENetwork%20Change%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20every%20other%20active%20site%20on%20the%20account%2C%20summed.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EThe%20%22control%20group%22%20is%20a%20small%20curated%20sample%20(e.g.%20Top%205%20or%20LMS%20Core)%3B%20the%20network%20is%20the%20%3Cem%3Ewhole%20rest%20of%20the%20account%3C%2Fem%3E.%20It%5C's%20the%20broadest%20possible%20ambient-traffic%20reference.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Useful%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20if%20the%20network%20rose%20while%20your%20target%20fell%2C%20the%20fix%20is%20clearly%20site-specific%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Warning%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20if%20the%20network%20fell%20by%20a%20similar%20amount%2C%20much%20of%20your%20target%5C's%20improvement%20may%20just%20be%20a%20quiet%20week'%2Cnet_effect%3A'%3Cstrong%3ENet%20Effect%20(Difference-in-Differences)%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20the%20target%5C's%20%25%20change%20%3Cem%3Eminus%3C%2Fem%3E%20the%20network%5C's%20%25%20change.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EThis%20is%20the%20single%20number%20that%20answers%20%22did%20my%20fix%20actually%20do%20anything%3F%22%20%E2%80%94%20it%20removes%20the%20ambient%20drift%20that%20hit%20every%20site.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Strong%20fix%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20strongly%20negative%20(e.g.%20-25%20pp)%20%E2%80%94%20target%20dropped%20much%20more%20than%20the%20network%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%86%92%20Noise%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20near%200%20%E2%80%94%20target%20moved%20with%20the%20network%2C%20no%20isolated%20effect%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Regression%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20positive%20%E2%80%94%20target%20got%20worse%20relative%20to%20peers%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3EReported%20in%20percentage%20points%2C%20not%20%25.%20A%20net%20effect%20of%20-20%20pp%20means%20the%20target%20fell%2020%20percentage%20points%20more%20than%20the%20network%20did.%3C%2Fem%3E'%2Cexpected_after%3A'%3Cstrong%3EExpected%20After%20(Counterfactual)%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20what%20the%20target%5C's%20after-CPU%20would%20be%20if%20it%20had%20drifted%20at%20the%20same%20rate%20as%20the%20rest%20of%20the%20network.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EComparing%20%3Cem%3Eactual%20after%3C%2Fem%3E%20against%20this%20number%20is%20the%20cleanest%20single-number%20%22savings%22%20estimate.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Saved%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20actual%20%3C%20expected%20%E2%80%94%20the%20fix%20kept%20CPU%20below%20what%20ambient%20drift%20alone%20predicted%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Lost%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20actual%20%3E%20expected%20%E2%80%94%20the%20target%20underperformed%20peers'%2Csite_rank%3A'%3Cstrong%3ESite%20Rank%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20where%20the%20target%20sits%20among%20all%20active%20sites%20when%20ranked%20by%20before%E2%86%92after%20%25%20change.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ERank%201%20%3D%20biggest%20improvement%20(largest%20CPU%20drop).%20Rank%20N%20%3D%20biggest%20regression.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EThe%20percentile%20reframes%20that%20as%20%22the%20target%20improved%20more%20than%20X%25%20of%20sites.%22%20Combined%20with%20the%20network%20change%2C%20it%20tells%20you%20whether%20your%20target%20was%20uniquely%20improved%20or%20merely%20went%20along%20for%20the%20ride.'%2Cplan_change%3A'%3Cstrong%3EPlan%20Change%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20the%20date%20SiteGround%20upgraded%20or%20downgraded%20your%20account%5C's%20cores%20or%20memory.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EDetected%20automatically%20from%20%3Ccode%3Elimits_list%3C%2Fcode%3E%20in%20the%20API%20response.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Every%20metric%20on%20this%20dashboard%20is%20plan-immune%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20CPU%20seconds%2C%20executions%2C%20cores%20in%20use%2C%20and%20GB%20used%20are%20all%20absolute%20measurements.%20A%20day%20that%20used%201.47%20cores%20is%20shown%20as%201.47%20cores%20regardless%20of%20whether%20the%20plan%20was%207-core%20or%209-core%20on%20that%20day.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%2327aae1%22%3E%E2%84%B9%EF%B8%8F%20What%20changes%20after%20the%20upgrade%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20the%20%22%25%20of%20plan%22%20subtitle%20next%20to%20each%20absolute%20reading%20%E2%80%94%201.47%20cores%20was%2021%25%20of%207-core%20plan%2C%20now%20it%5C's%2016.3%25%20of%209-core%20plan.%20The%20cores%20number%20is%20the%20same%3B%20the%20%25%20framing%20scales.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3EThe%20purple%20marker%20on%20charts%20is%20purely%20informational.%3C%2Fem%3E'%2Ccore_peak%3A'%3Cstrong%3EDaily%20Peak%20Cores%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20the%20single%20highest%201-hour%20cores-in-use%20reading%20for%20that%20day.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EDifferent%20from%20the%20daily%20%3Cem%3Eaverage%3C%2Fem%3E%20SiteGround%20reports%20as%20the%20%22daily%22%20value%3A%20a%20day%20with%202.8%20cores%20peak%20and%200.95%20cores%20average%20is%20a%20bursty%20day%2C%20not%20a%20quiet%20one.%20The%20peak%20is%20what%20determines%20whether%20users%20felt%20slowdowns.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EDerived%20from%20the%20hourly%20stream%20%E2%80%94%20needs%20hourly%20data%20captured%20(open%20SG%20Statistics%20%E2%86%92%20Last%2024%20Hours%20to%20populate).'%2Cmem_combined%3A'%3Cstrong%3ECombined%20Memory%20(real%20%2B%20cache)%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20process%20memory%20plus%20filesystem%20cache%20as%20a%20%25%20of%20plan%20limit.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3EReal%3C%2Fem%3E%20is%20what%20gets%20OOM-killed%3B%20%3Cem%3Ecache%3C%2Fem%3E%20is%20reclaimable.%20But%20once%20combined%20memory%20exceeds%20~75%25%20the%20OS%20starts%20evicting%20cache%2C%20page%20faults%20rise%2C%20and%20any%20growth%20in%20real%20risks%20an%20out-of-memory%20event.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Safe%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20%26lt%3B60%25%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%20Watch%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%2060%E2%80%9375%25%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Pressure%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20%26gt%3B75%25%20%E2%80%94%20even%20though%20%22real%22%20alone%20may%20look%20fine.'%2Cburst_score%3A'%3Cstrong%3EBurst%20Score%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20peak-hour%20CPU%20as%20a%20%25%20of%20the%20site%5C's%20last-24h%20total.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ETells%20you%20whether%20a%20site%20is%20bursty%20(one%20bad%20hour%20does%20all%20the%20damage)%20or%20steady%20(organic%20spread%20across%20the%20day).%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Steady%3A%20%26lt%3B10%25%3C%2Fspan%3E%20%E2%80%94%20typical%20organic%20traffic%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%20Spiky%3A%2010%E2%80%9325%25%3C%2Fspan%3E%20%E2%80%94%20heavy%20single-hour%20peaks%2C%20often%20scheduled%20tasks%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Burst%3A%20%26gt%3B25%25%3C%2Fspan%3E%20%E2%80%94%20one%20cron%20job%20or%20batch%20process%20is%20the%20whole%20story'%2Cactivity_class%3A%22%3Cstrong%3EActivity%20Class%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20categorical%20change%20for%20the%20site%20over%20the%20comparison%20window.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cstrong%3ENEW%3C%2Fstrong%3E%3A%20was%200%20before%2C%20has%20traffic%20now%3Cbr%3E%3Cstrong%3EDIED%3C%2Fstrong%3E%3A%20had%20traffic%2C%20now%200%3Cbr%3E%3Cstrong%3EGREW%3C%2Fstrong%3E%3A%20CPU%20rose%20%26gt%3B25%25%3Cbr%3E%3Cstrong%3ESHRANK%3C%2Fstrong%3E%3A%20CPU%20fell%20%26gt%3B25%25%3Cbr%3E%3Cstrong%3ESTABLE%3C%2Fstrong%3E%3A%20within%20%C2%B125%25%3Cbr%3E%3Cstrong%3EINACTIVE%3C%2Fstrong%3E%3A%20zero%20in%20both%20windows%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EUsed%20to%20filter%20the%20all-sites%20ranking%20so%20a%20site%20going%201%E2%86%92100%20doesn't%20spam%20the%20leaderboard%20with%20a%20%2B9900%25%20non-result.%22%2Ccpu_exec_growth%3A%22%3Cstrong%3ECPU%20vs%20Exec%20Growth%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20did%20each%20request%20get%20cheaper%20or%20heavier%20across%20the%20window%3F%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EEqual%20CPU%20and%20exec%20growth%20%3D%20same%20per-request%20cost.%20CPU%20grew%20faster%20than%20exec%20%3D%20each%20call%20is%20now%20more%20expensive%20(heavier%20code%20path%2C%20bigger%20queries%2C%20slower%20endpoints).%20Exec%20grew%20faster%20than%20CPU%20%3D%20each%20call%20is%20cheaper%20(optimisation%2C%20cache%20warmth%2C%20lighter%20routes).%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EBest%20fix%20signal%3A%20exec%20stays%20similar%2C%20per-request%20cost%20drops%20%E2%80%94%20same%20traffic%2C%20each%20hit%20cheaper.%22%2Ccredibility%3A'%3Cstrong%3ECredibility%20Score%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20(0%E2%80%93100)%20%E2%80%94%20how%20trustworthy%20this%20Before%2FAfter%20analysis%20is%2C%20given%20the%20data%20we%20have.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ECombines%3A%20window%20length%20(%E2%89%A57%20days%20both%20sides%20best)%2C%20weekday-pair%20coverage%20(matching%20weekdays%20before%20vs%20after)%2C%20plan-change-straddle%20(huge%20negative%20if%20window%20crosses%20an%20upgrade)%2C%20and%20statistical%20significance%20of%20the%20DiD%20residuals.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20High%20(75%E2%80%93100)%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20safe%20to%20present%20to%20management%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%20Moderate%20(50%E2%80%9374)%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20directional%2C%20not%20conclusive%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Low%20(%26lt%3B50)%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20add%20more%20data%20before%20drawing%20conclusions'%2Cweekday_paired%3A%22%3Cstrong%3EWeekday-Paired%20Analysis%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20compares%20before%20vs%20after%20using%20only%20days%20that%20match%20weekdays%20in%20both%20windows.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ERemoves%20the%20seasonality%20bias%20where%20a%20before-window%20heavy%20on%20weekdays%20vs%20an%20after-window%20with%20weekends%20would%20look%20like%20a%20fix%20when%20it's%20just%20calendar%20mix.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EPair%20count%20%3D%20how%20many%20distinct%20weekdays%20got%20matched.%207%20%3D%20ideal%20(full%20week%20represented%20on%20both%20sides)%3B%20fewer%20%3D%20less%20robust.%22%2Cdid_residual%3A%22%3Cstrong%3EDiD%20Residual%20t-test%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20Welch's%20t-test%20on%20per-day%20differences%20between%20the%20target%20and%20what%20the%20network's%20growth%20rate%20predicts%20for%20it.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EWhere%20the%20headline%20Welch's%20t-test%20asks%20%5C%22did%20the%20target's%20number%20change%3F%5C%22%2C%20this%20asks%20%5C%22did%20the%20target%20deviate%20from%20network%20drift%3F%5C%22%20That's%20the%20more%20honest%20statistical%20test%20of%20the%20fix's%20isolated%20effect.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3Ep%20%26lt%3B%200.05%20%3D%20the%20deviation%20is%20unlikely%20to%20be%20random%20noise.%22%2Cheadroom%3A'%3Cstrong%3ECores%20in%20Use%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20how%20many%20of%20your%20plan%5C's%20cores%20are%20currently%20doing%20work%2C%20out%20of%20the%20total.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E0.6%20%2F%209%20means%200.6%20cores%20are%20in%20use%20and%208.4%20are%20free.%20We%20lead%20with%20the%20absolute%20number%20because%20%22how%20fast%20we%5C're%20going%22%20is%20the%20useful%20question%20for%20analysing%20workload%3B%20the%20%22%25%20of%20plan%22%20framing%20matters%20only%20for%20capacity%20planning%20(%22will%20we%20need%20to%20upgrade%3F%22).%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ECores%20in%20use%20is%20%3Cstrong%3Eplan-immune%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20the%20number%20doesn%5C't%20change%20just%20because%20SG%20bumped%20you%20from%20a%207-core%20to%20a%209-core%20plan.'%2Cdead_site%3A%22%3Cstrong%3EDead%20%2F%20Parked%20Site%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20a%20site%20on%20the%20account%20with%20zero%20CPU%20activity%20over%20the%20captured%20period.%20Likely%20staging%2C%20suspended%2C%20or%20pointing%20elsewhere.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EDashed%20out%20by%20default%20to%20keep%20rankings%20honest.%20Surface%20them%20via%20the%20All-Sites%20table%20if%20you%20want%20to%20confirm.%22%2Ctarget_share%3A'%3Cstrong%3ETarget%20Share%20of%20Account%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20the%20target%20site%5C's%20CPU%20as%20a%20%25%20of%20the%20entire%20server%20total.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EImmune%20to%20traffic%20fluctuations.%20If%20the%20server%20gets%20quieter%20overall%2C%20shares%20stay%20the%20same.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Fix%20confirmed%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20target%5C's%20share%20drops%20%E2%80%94%20proportionally%20cheaper%20vs%20everything%20else%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Problem%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20target%5C's%20share%20growing%20%E2%80%94%20consuming%20increasing%20fraction%20of%20shared%20resources%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3EExample%3A%20site%20drops%20from%2012%25%20%E2%86%92%205%25%20of%20server%20CPU%20%E2%80%94%20a%20real%20fix%20regardless%20of%20traffic%20levels.%3C%2Fem%3E'%2Ctarget_ratio%3A'%3Cstrong%3ETarget%20%2F%20Control%20Ratio%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20target%20CPU%20as%20a%20%25%20of%20the%20control%20group%5C's%20combined%20CPU.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EThe%20gold%20standard%20for%20isolating%20fix%20effectiveness%20from%20background%20noise.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Fix%20confirmed%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20ratio%20drops%20significantly%20%E2%80%94%20target%20improved%20more%20than%20its%20peers%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20No%20effect%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20ratio%20unchanged%20%E2%80%94%20fix%20had%20no%20isolated%20impact%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3EExample%3A%20ratio%2014%25%20%E2%86%92%206.5%25%20while%20control%20rose%2018%25.%20Fix%20was%20causal%2C%20not%20ambient.%3C%2Fem%3E'%2Ctrend7%3A'%3Cstrong%3E7-Day%20Trend%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20last%207%20days%20vs%20the%207%20days%20before%20that.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Improving%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20negative%20%25%20(CPU%20reducing%20week-on-week)%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%2364748b%22%3E%E2%86%92%20Stable%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20near%200%25%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Worsening%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20strongly%20positive%20%E2%80%94%20CPU%20climbing%20week-on-week%2C%20investigate%20before%20it%20causes%20incidents'%2Cpeak_day%3A'%3Cstrong%3EPeak%20Day%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20single%20highest%20daily%20CPU%20value%20in%20the%20period.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EPeaks%20matter%20more%20than%20averages%20for%20server%20stability.%20A%20site%20averaging%205%2C000%20but%20peaking%20at%2050%2C000%20can%20briefly%20saturate%20the%20server%20for%20all%20other%20sites.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Stable%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20peak%20within%202%E2%80%933%C3%97%20daily%20average%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%20Spiky%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20peak%205%E2%80%9310%C3%97%20average%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Unstable%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20peak%2010%C3%97%2B%20average%20%E2%80%94%20runaway%20process%20or%20traffic%20incident'%2Cincomplete_day%3A%22%3Cstrong%3EPartial%20Day%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20today's%20data%20is%20still%20accumulating.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EFlagged%20when%20total%20CPU%20is%20less%20than%2025%25%20of%20the%207-day%20rolling%20average.%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3EPartial%20days%20are%20automatically%20excluded%20from%20all%20averages%2C%20benchmarks%2C%20and%20before%2Fafter%20calculations.%3C%2Fem%3E%22%2Cfix_date%3A%22%3Cstrong%3EFix%20%2F%20Event%20Date%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20the%20dividing%20line%20between%20before%20and%20after.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EChoose%20the%20date%20you%20deployed%20the%20fix%20or%20applied%20a%20config%20change.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cem%3ETip%3A%20wait%205%E2%80%937%20complete%20days%20after%20the%20fix%20before%20presenting%20results.%20With%202%E2%80%933%20days%2C%20one%20high-traffic%20day%20in%20the%20after%20window%20can%20skew%20the%20comparison%20significantly.%3C%2Fem%3E%22%2Caccount_total%3A'%3Cstrong%3EAccount%20Total%20CPU%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20combined%20CPU%20seconds%20for%20all%20sites%20on%20your%20account.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Good%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20stable%20or%20trending%20down%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Problem%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20spiking%20%E2%80%94%20one%20site%20likely%20out%20of%20control%3B%20check%20shares%20to%20identify%20which%20one'%2Ccpu_exec_ratio%3A'%3Cstrong%3ECPU%20per%20Execution%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20average%20CPU%20seconds%20spent%20per%20PHP%20invocation.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EIsolates%20whether%20a%20fix%20reduced%20load%20%3Cem%3Evolume%3C%2Fem%3E%20(fewer%20requests)%20or%20%3Cem%3Ecost%3C%2Fem%3E%20(each%20request%20cheaper).%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Reduced%20overhead%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20ratio%20drops%20%E2%80%94%20each%20call%20costs%20less%20(e.g.%20early-intercept%20bypassing%20PHP%20bootstrap)%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%86%92%20Reduced%20volume%20only%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20ratio%20same%2C%20executions%20down%20%E2%80%94%20requests%20blocked%20before%20starting%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20Worse%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20ratio%20rises%20%E2%80%94%20fewer%20requests%20but%20each%20heavier%2C%20worth%20investigating'%2Chealth_score%3A'%3Cstrong%3EHealth%20Score%20(0%E2%80%93100)%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20composite%20server%20condition%20indicator.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3ECombines%3A%20live%20cores%20in%20use%20vs%20plan%20limit%2C%20recent%20CPU%20trend%20vs%207-day%20average%2C%20per-site%20anomalies%20from%20hourly%20data%2C%20live%20memory%20vs%20plan%20limit%2C%20and%20site-concentration%20(HHI).%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%2075%E2%80%93100%3A%20Healthy%3C%2Fspan%3E%20%E2%80%94%20operating%20normally%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%2045%E2%80%9374%3A%20Elevated%3C%2Fspan%3E%20%E2%80%94%20some%20load%20pressure%2C%20monitor%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%200%E2%80%9344%3A%20Under%20Pressure%3C%2Fspan%3E%20%E2%80%94%20investigate%20immediately'%2Cbarometer%3A'%3Cstrong%3ELoad%20Barometer%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20%E2%80%94%20real-time%20server%20pressure%20at%2015-second%20resolution.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3EThe%20last%2030%20minutes%20of%20cores-in-use%20%E2%80%94%20the%20fastest%20available%20signal%20of%20server%20stress.%20Thresholds%20scale%20with%20your%20plan%20size%3A%2075%25%20of%20plan%20is%20the%20saturation%20line%20regardless%20of%20whether%20the%20plan%20is%207%20or%209%20cores.%3Cbr%3E%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%234ade80%22%3E%E2%9C%85%20Low%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20steady%20below%2050%25%20of%20plan%20%E2%80%94%20server%20cruising%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23fbbf24%22%3E%E2%9A%A0%EF%B8%8F%20Elevated%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20trending%20toward%2075%25%20%E2%80%94%20monitor%20next%2030%20min%3Cbr%3E%3Cspan%20style%3D%22color%3A%23f87171%22%3E%F0%9F%94%B4%20High%3A%3C%2Fspan%3E%20sustained%2075%25%2B%20%E2%80%94%20users%20experiencing%20slowdowns%20right%20now'%7D%2Ca%3D%5B%7Bsection%3A%22Capacity%22%2Citems%3A%5B%7Bkey%3A%22cpu_seconds%22%2Ctitle%3A%22CPU%20Seconds%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20much%20processing%20power%20your%20sites%20used%20%E2%80%94%20think%20of%20it%20as%20an%20electricity%20meter%20for%20the%20server.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Sum%20of%20CPU%20time%20consumed%20by%20all%20PHP%2C%20MySQL%2C%20cron%2C%20and%20background%20processes%20on%20the%20account%2C%20in%20seconds.%20SG%20reports%20per-site%20totals%20plus%20an%20account%20total.%20We%20dedupe%20SG's%20double-emit%20(real%20value%20%2B%200%20sentinel%20per%20day)%20using%20%3Ccode%3EMath.max%3C%2Fcode%3E%20per%20date.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22When%20SiteGround%20warns%20you're%20approaching%20the%20CPU-seconds%20plan%20ceiling%2C%20or%20when%20you%20want%20to%20know%20which%20sites%20are%20doing%20the%20most%20work.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Health%20%E2%86%92%20%22Live%20Hourly%20CPU%22%20%C2%B7%20Sites%20%E2%86%92%20%22Total%20CPU%22%20column%20%C2%B7%20Trends%20%E2%86%92%20%22CPU%20Seconds%20%E2%80%94%20Daily%22'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22program_executions%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Program%20Executions%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20many%20times%20PHP%20actually%20ran.%20Each%20page%20load%2C%20AJAX%20call%2C%20or%20cron%20tick%20counts%20as%20one.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Count%20of%20PHP-FPM%20child%20invocations%20across%20the%20account.%20Strong%20correlate%20of%20request%20volume%2C%20but%20doesn't%20include%20static-file%20hits%20served%20by%20Nginx.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'To%20distinguish%20%22more%20traffic%22%20from%20%22heavier%20traffic%22.%20Combined%20with%20CPU%20Seconds%20it%20tells%20you%20whether%20each%20request%20is%20getting%20cheaper%20or%20more%20expensive.'%2CwhereToFind%3A'Sites%20%E2%86%92%20%22Exec%22%20column%20%C2%B7%20Trends%20%E2%86%92%20%22Program%20Executions%20%E2%80%94%20Daily%22%20%C2%B7%20Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20%22Volume%20vs%20Cost%22'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22core_pct%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Cores%20in%20Use%20(Server%20CPU)%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20many%20CPU%20cores%20the%20whole%20server%20is%20using%20right%20now.%200.6%20cores%20out%20of%209%20means%20the%20server%20is%20barely%20doing%20anything%3B%207%20out%20of%209%20means%20it's%20under%20pressure.%20The%20number%20is%20the%20same%20whether%20your%20plan%20is%207%20or%209%20cores%20%E2%80%94%20it%20measures%20actual%20work%2C%20not%20capacity.%22%2Ctechnical%3A'SiteGround%20returns%20the%20raw%20value%20as%20%22%25%20of%20one%20core%22%20(0%E2%80%93900%25%20on%20a%209-core%20plan).%20We%20divide%20by%20100%20to%20get%20cores%20in%20use.%20SG%20samples%20at%2015-second%20resolution.%20The%20%22daily%22%20value%20SG%20returns%20is%20the%20day%5C's%20average%20(NOT%20the%20peak)%3B%20we%20derive%20a%20peak-from-hourly%20separately.%20Cores%20in%20use%20is%20plan-immune%20by%20construction%20%E2%80%94%20comparable%20across%20plan%20changes%20without%20normalisation.'%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22When%20users%20are%20reporting%20slowness.%20Sustained%20%E2%89%A575%25%20of%20plan%20capacity%20means%20requests%20are%20queueing%20and%20people%20feel%20it.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Health%20%E2%86%92%20%22Cores%20in%20Use%22%20gauge%20%26%20headroom%20%C2%B7%20Trends%20%E2%86%92%20%22Cores%20in%20Use%22%20daily%20chart%20(avg%20%2B%20peak%20overlay)'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22core_peak%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Daily%20Peak%20Cores%22%2Clayman%3A%22The%20worst%20hour%20on%20each%20day%20%E2%80%94%20how%20spiky%20the%20server%20got%2C%20not%20just%20the%20average.%22%2Ctechnical%3A'%3Ccode%3Emax(core_hourly)%20%2F%20100%3C%2Fcode%3E%20per%20date%20%E2%80%94%20cores%20in%20use%20at%20the%20hour%20of%20peak.%20SG%5C's%20%22daily%22%20value%20is%20an%20average%20and%20can%20hide%20a%20sharp%20intraday%20spike.%20Requires%20hourly%20capture.'%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Catching%20brief%20overload%20bursts%20that%20an%20averaging%20chart%20would%20smooth%20out.%20Useful%20when%20one%20bad%20scheduled%20job%20or%20traffic%20spike%20causes%20user-reported%20slowness.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Trends%20%E2%86%92%20%22Cores%20in%20Use%20%E2%80%94%20Daily%22%20(dashed%20amber%20line)'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22mem_combined%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Combined%20Memory%20(Real%20%2B%20Cache)%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20much%20of%20the%20server's%20memory%20is%20in%20use%2C%20including%20the%20OS's%20working%20files.%20The%20honest%20%5C%22are%20we%20close%20to%20running%20out%3F%5C%22%20number.%22%2Ctechnical%3A'%3Ccode%3Ereal%20GB%20%2B%20cache%20GB%3C%2Fcode%3E%2C%20compared%20against%20%3Ccode%3Elimit%20GB%3C%2Fcode%3E.%20Real%20%3D%20process%20memory%20(the%20kind%20that%20gets%20OOM-killed).%20Cache%20%3D%20filesystem%20buffer%20(reclaimable%20but%20not%20free).%20Above%20~75%25%20of%20plan%20the%20OS%20starts%20evicting%20cache%20and%20risks%20an%20OOM%20if%20real%20grows.%20Display%20leads%20with%20GB%20used%3B%20%22%25%20of%20plan%22%20shown%20as%20subtitle.'%2CwhenItMatters%3A'When%20the%20dashboard%5C's%20%22Real%22%20alone%20looks%20fine%20(e.g.%204%20GB%20of%2014)%20but%20combined%20is%20at%2011%20GB%20%E2%80%94%20your%20server%20has%20less%20headroom%20than%20it%20appears.'%2CwhereToFind%3A'Health%20%E2%86%92%20%22Memory%20in%20Use%22%20headroom%20%C2%B7%20Trends%20%E2%86%92%20%22Memory%20(GB)%22%20combined%20line'%2Cseverity%3A%22good%20%3C%2060%25%20of%20plan%20%C2%B7%20watch%2060%E2%80%9375%25%20%C2%B7%20pressure%20%3E%2075%25%20(rule%20of%20thumb%2C%20not%20from%20SG%20docs)%22%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22headroom%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Headroom%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20much%20you've%20got%20left%20right%20now%20%E2%80%94%20cores%20free%2C%20GB%20free.%20Inverse%20of%20utilisation%20but%20framed%20as%20capacity%20remaining.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Plan%20limit%20minus%20current%20consumption.%20Cores%20in%20use%20is%20plan-immune%2C%20GB%20used%20is%20plan-immune%20%E2%80%94%20both%20are%20absolute%20work%20measurements.%20Headroom%20is%20the%20same%20currency%3A%208.4%20of%209%20cores%20free%20is%20meaningful%20regardless%20of%20which%20plan%20size%20that%20is.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'Pricing%2Fcapacity%20conversations%20with%20stakeholders.%20%22We%20have%208%20cores%20free%20during%20peak%22%20is%20more%20honest%20than%20%22we%5C're%20at%2011%25%20average%22.'%2CwhereToFind%3A%22Health%20%E2%86%92%20headroom%20strip%20(top%20of%20the%20tab)%22%7D%5D%7D%2C%7Bsection%3A%22Per-site%22%2Citems%3A%5B%7Bkey%3A%22cpu_exec_ratio%22%2Ctitle%3A%22CPU%20per%20Execution%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20heavy%20each%20request%20was%20on%20average.%20Lower%20is%20better%20%E2%80%94%20it%20means%20each%20page%2FAPI%20call%20cost%20less.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22%3Ccode%3Ecpu_seconds%20%2F%20program_executions%3C%2Fcode%3E%20at%20the%20same%20granularity.%20Captures%20cost-per-request%20without%20conflating%20volume%20changes.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Comparing%20fixes%3A%20if%20executions%20stayed%20the%20same%20but%20CPU%20dropped%2C%20each%20request%20got%20cheaper%20(a%20clean%20optimization).%20If%20executions%20dropped%20and%20per-exec%20stayed%20the%20same%2C%20you%20blocked%20traffic.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Sites%20%E2%86%92%20%22CPU%2FExec%22%20column%20%C2%B7%20Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20%22Volume%20vs%20Cost%22%20chart'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22burst_score%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Burst%20Score%20(last%2024h)%22%2Clayman%3A%22Did%20one%20bad%20hour%20cause%20all%20the%20trouble%20today%2C%20or%20was%20it%20spread%20out%20evenly%3F%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22%3Ccode%3Epeak_hour_cpu%20%2F%2024h_total%3C%2Fcode%3E.%20A%20score%20of%2025%25%20means%20one%20hour%20did%20a%20quarter%20of%20the%20day's%20work%20%E2%80%94%20typical%20of%20cron%20jobs%20or%20scheduled%20traffic.%208%25%20or%20less%20%3D%20steady%20organic.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Hunting%20for%20misbehaving%20scheduled%20tasks%20or%20daily%20traffic%20peaks.%20Burst%20score%20%3E25%25%20%E2%86%92%20check%20what%20runs%20at%20that%20hour.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Sites%20%E2%86%92%20%22Burst%2024h%22%20column'%2Cseverity%3A%22steady%20%3C%2010%25%20%C2%B7%20spiky%2010%E2%80%9325%25%20%C2%B7%20burst%20%3E%2025%25%22%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22activity_class%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Activity%20Class%20(Before%2FAfter)%22%2Clayman%3A%22A%20simple%20label%20for%20how%20a%20site%20changed%20during%20your%20comparison%20window%3A%20stable%2C%20grew%2C%20shrank%2C%20just%20woke%20up%2C%20or%20went%20dark.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22NEW%3A%200%20before%2C%20%26gt%3B0%20after.%20DIED%3A%20%26gt%3B0%20before%2C%200%20after.%20GREW%2FSHRANK%3A%20%C2%B125%25%20threshold.%20STABLE%3A%20within%20%C2%B125%25.%20INACTIVE%3A%200%20in%20both.%20NEW%2FDIED%2FINACTIVE%20are%20pulled%20out%20of%20the%20ranking%20because%20their%20%25-change%20is%20undefined%20or%20meaningless.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'Stops%20a%20tiny%20site%20going%201%E2%86%92100%20CPU%20from%20dominating%20a%20%22%2B9900%25%22%20leaderboard.%20Surfaces%20newly-launched%20and%20recently-killed%20sites%20separately.'%2CwhereToFind%3A'Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20%22All-Sites%20Ranking%22%20%E2%86%92%20Activity%20column'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22cpu_exec_growth%22%2Ctitle%3A%22CPU%20vs%20Exec%20Growth%22%2Clayman%3A%22Did%20each%20request%20get%20cheaper%20or%20more%20expensive%20over%20the%20comparison%20window%3F%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22%3Ccode%3E%25%CE%94%20CPU%20%E2%88%92%20%25%CE%94%20executions%3C%2Fcode%3E.%20Positive%20%3D%20each%20request%20is%20more%20expensive%20now%20(heavier%20code%20path%2C%20bigger%20queries).%20Negative%20%3D%20each%20request%20is%20cheaper%20(caching%2C%20optimization).%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'Reading%20the%20result%20of%20a%20fix%20more%20cleanly.%20Watch%20for%20the%20%22executions%20fell%2C%20CPU%2Fexec%20rose%22%20pattern%3A%20it%20usually%20means%20the%20cheap%20requests%20got%20blocked%20while%20expensive%20ones%20still%20run.'%2CwhereToFind%3A'Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20%22All-Sites%20Ranking%22%20%E2%86%92%20%22Per-req%20%CE%94%25%22%20column'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22dead_site%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Dead%20%2F%20Parked%20Site%22%2Clayman%3A%22A%20site%20that%20exists%20in%20your%20account%20but%20isn't%20doing%20anything%20%E2%80%94%20usually%20staging%2C%20suspended%2C%20or%20pointed%20elsewhere.%22%2Ctechnical%3A'Zero%20CPU%20consumption%20across%20the%20entire%20captured%20window.%20Excluded%20from%20rankings%20(no%20signal).%20%22Quiet%22%20%3D%20active%20fewer%20than%203%20days.'%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Keeps%20the%20network-comparison%20maths%20honest%20and%20the%20rankings%20de-cluttered.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Sites%20tab%20%E2%86%92%20grey%20dashed%20%22dead%22%20badge'%7D%5D%7D%2C%7Bsection%3A%22Before%2FAfter%20analytics%22%2Citems%3A%5B%7Bkey%3A%22control_group%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Control%20Group%22%2Clayman%3A%22A%20hand-picked%20set%20of%20unrelated%20sites%20to%20compare%20against.%20If%20they%20got%20quieter%20at%20the%20same%20time%20your%20target%20did%2C%20the%20fix%20didn't%20cause%20it%20%E2%80%94%20a%20quiet%20week%20did.%22%2Ctechnical%3A'Selectable%20preset%3A%20Auto-top-5%2C%20%22Unpatched%22%20(snn%2Beinn)%2C%20LMS%20Core%204%2C%20Non-LMS.%20Frozen%20on%20first%20run%20with%20auto-mode%20so%20the%20comparison%20stays%20stable%20across%20re-renders.'%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Sense-checking%20a%20single-site%20improvement.%20A%20target%20that%20drops%2030%25%20means%20nothing%20if%20the%20control%20also%20dropped%2030%25.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20%22Control%20Avg%20Daily%22%20card%20%2B%20green-dashed%20line%20on%20the%20main%20chart'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22network_change%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Network%20Change%22%2Clayman%3A'Same%20idea%20as%20control%20group%2C%20but%20using%20EVERY%20other%20site%20on%20the%20account%20%E2%80%94%20the%20broadest%20possible%20%22what%20did%20the%20whole%20rest%20of%20the%20network%20do%3F%22%20baseline.'%2Ctechnical%3A%22Sum%20of%20CPU%20across%20every%20active%20site%20except%20the%20target%2C%20before%20and%20after%20the%20fix%20date.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'A%20%22control%20group%22%20can%20be%20cherry-picked.%20The%20network%20is%20everyone%20else%20%E2%80%94%20much%20harder%20to%20argue%20with.'%2CwhereToFind%3A'Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20Net%20Effect%20hero%20card%20%E2%86%92%20%22Network%20Change%22%20column'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22net_effect%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Net%20Effect%20(Difference-in-Differences)%22%2Clayman%3A'The%20single%20most%20honest%20answer%20to%20%22did%20the%20fix%20actually%20do%20anything%3F%22%20It%20strips%20out%20whatever%20was%20happening%20to%20the%20rest%20of%20the%20network.'%2Ctechnical%3A%22Target%20%25-change%20%E2%88%92%20Network%20%25-change%2C%20in%20percentage%20points%20(pp).%20%E2%88%9215pp%20%3D%20target%20dropped%2015pp%20more%20than%20peers.%20Near%200%20%3D%20target%20moved%20with%20the%20herd.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'The%20headline%20number%20for%20presenting%20fix%20results%20to%20stakeholders.%20Combined%20with%20the%20credibility%20score%2C%20it%5C's%20the%20closest%20you%20get%20to%20a%20%22did%20this%20work%3F%22%20verdict%20from%20CPU%20data%20alone.'%2CwhereToFind%3A'Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20%22Net%20Effect%22%20hero%20card'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22expected_after%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Expected%20After%20(Counterfactual)%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20much%20CPU%20your%20target%20would%20be%20using%20if%20it%20had%20just%20drifted%20with%20the%20rest%20of%20the%20network%20%E2%80%94%20i.e.%2C%20if%20the%20fix%20had%20done%20nothing.%22%2Ctechnical%3A'%3Ccode%3EtAvgBefore%20%C3%97%20(1%20%2B%20networkPctCh%2F100)%3C%2Fcode%3E.%20Compare%20to%20actual%20after-CPU%20to%20see%20%22real%20saving%22%20vs%20%22ambient%20drift%22.'%2CwhenItMatters%3A'Converting%20a%20percentage%20win%20into%20a%20concrete%20number%3A%20%22we%20saved%208%2C400%20CPU%20sec%2Fday%20vs.%20the%20do-nothing%20counterfactual%22%20is%20more%20concrete%20than%20%22%E2%88%9218%25%22.'%2CwhereToFind%3A%22Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20Net%20Effect%20card%20%E2%86%92%20bottom%20row%22%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22weekday_paired%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Weekday-Paired%20Analysis%22%2Clayman%3A'Compares%20Mondays%20to%20Mondays%2C%20Tuesdays%20to%20Tuesdays%2C%20etc.%20%E2%80%94%20so%20a%20weekend-heavy%20%22before%22%20doesn%5C't%20make%20a%20weekday-heavy%20%22after%22%20look%20better%20than%20it%20really%20is.'%2Ctechnical%3A%22Each%20after-day%20is%20matched%20to%20a%20before-day%20with%20the%20same%20UTC%20weekday%3B%20unmatched%20days%20are%20dropped.%20Net%20effect%20computed%20only%20on%20the%20matched%20pairs.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Any%20time%20your%20before%2Fafter%20windows%20have%20different%20calendar%20mixes%20(very%20common%20with%20short%20windows%20around%20weekends%20or%20bank%20holidays).%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20%22Weekday-paired%20DiD%22%20card'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22did_residual%22%2Ctitle%3A%22DiD%20Residual%20t-test%22%2Clayman%3A'Asks%20the%20right%20statistical%20question%3A%20%22did%20the%20target%20deviate%20from%20network%20drift%20more%20than%20random%20noise%20can%20explain%3F%22%20Not%20the%20same%20as%20%22did%20the%20target%5C's%20number%20change%3F%22'%2Ctechnical%3A%22Welch's%20t-test%20comparing%20the%20target's%20zero-mean%20before-residuals%20to%20its%20after-residuals%20after%20applying%20the%20network%20growth%20factor.%20p%20%26lt%3B%200.05%20%3D%20isolated%20effect%20is%20statistically%20significant.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22When%20defending%20a%20fix%20to%20skeptical%20audiences.%20The%20raw%20Welch's%20t-test%20on%20the%20target%20alone%20can%20be%20significant%20due%20to%20network%20drift%3B%20this%20test%20screens%20that%20out.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A%22Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20Weekday-paired%20DiD%20card%20%E2%86%92%20bottom%20row%22%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22site_rank%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Site%20Rank%20(All-Sites%20Ranking)%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20your%20target%20site%20ranked%20vs%20every%20other%20site%20on%20the%20account%2C%20sorted%20by%20who%20improved%20the%20most.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Sorted%20ascending%20by%20%25-change.%20Rank%201%20%3D%20biggest%20drop.%20Percentile%20%3D%20(N%20%E2%88%92%20rank)%20%2F%20(N%20%E2%88%92%201)%20%C3%97%20100.%20NEW%2FDIED%2FINACTIVE%20sites%20excluded.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Stress-testing%20the%20fix%20story.%20If%20your%20target%20is%20rank%205%20of%2020%20but%20most%20sites%20also%20improved%2C%20the%20network%20move%20dominated.%20If%20it's%20rank%201%20of%2020%20with%20the%20others%20flat%2C%20the%20fix%20did%20the%20work.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20%22All-Sites%20Ranking%22%20section'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22credibility%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Credibility%20Score%22%2Clayman%3A%22How%20trustworthy%20this%20whole%20Before%2FAfter%20analysis%20is.%20100%20%3D%20bulletproof%3B%20under%2050%20%3D%20collect%20more%20data%20first.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Composite%20of%3A%20days-after%20(%E2%89%A57%20%3D%2030pt%2C%205%20%3D%2022pt%2C%203%20%3D%2015pt)%20%2B%20days-before%20(%E2%89%A57%20%3D%2020pt%2C%20etc.)%20%2B%20distinct%20weekdays%20matched%20(20pt)%20%2B%20statistical%20significance%20bonus%20(20pt)%20%E2%88%92%20plan-change-straddle%20penalty%20(%E2%88%9225pt).%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'Stops%20you%20from%20presenting%20an%20%22obviously%20successful%20fix%22%20that%20was%20actually%20a%202-day%20fluke%20crossing%20a%20plan%20upgrade.'%2CwhereToFind%3A%22Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20Credibility%20card%20(top)%22%7D%5D%7D%2C%7Bsection%3A%22Trends%20%26%20projections%22%2Citems%3A%5B%7Bkey%3A%22trend7%22%2Ctitle%3A%227-Day%20Trend%22%2Clayman%3A%22Is%20this%20site%20getting%20busier%20or%20quieter%20this%20week%20compared%20to%20last%20week%3F%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22%3Ccode%3Eavg(last%207%20complete%20days)%20%2F%20avg(prior%207%20complete%20days)%20%E2%88%92%201%3C%2Fcode%3E%2C%20as%20a%20percentage.%20Partial%20today%20is%20excluded.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Early%20warning.%20A%20%2B50%25%20trend%20on%20a%20heavy%20site%20means%20investigate%20before%20the%20spike%20becomes%20an%20incident.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Sites%20%E2%86%92%20%227d%20Trend%22%20column%20%C2%B7%20Health%20%E2%86%92%20site%20health%20snapshot'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22core_pct_trajectory%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Cores%20Trajectory%22%2Clayman%3A%22A%20forecast%3A%20if%20the%20last%20week's%20trend%20keeps%20going%2C%20when%20will%20the%20server%20hit%2075%25%20of%20capacity%3F%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Linear%20regression%20on%20the%20last%207%20complete%20days%20of%20cores%20in%20use.%20Slope%20unit%20is%20cores%2Fday.%20%3Ccode%3Edays_to_75%20%3D%20(limit%20%C3%97%200.75%20%E2%88%92%20lastY)%20%2F%20slope%3C%2Fcode%3E.%2095%25%20CI%20from%20the%20residual%20standard%20error.%20Cores%20are%20plan-immune%20so%20the%20regression%20doesn't%20need%20normalisation.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'Capacity%20planning.%20%22We%20have%2030%20days%20at%20this%20rate%22%20vs%20%22we%20have%204%20days%22%20is%20the%20difference%20between%20%22monitor%22%20and%20%22act%20now%22.'%2CwhereToFind%3A'Health%20%E2%86%92%20headroom%20strip%20%E2%86%92%20%227d%20Trajectory%22%20%C2%B7%20Trends%20%E2%86%92%20bench-strip%20%E2%86%92%20Trajectory'%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22health_score%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Health%20Score%20(0%E2%80%93100)%22%2Clayman%3A%22One%20number%20summarising%20server%20condition%20right%20now%3A%20green%20%3D%20fine%2C%20amber%20%3D%20watch%2C%20red%20%3D%20act.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Weighted%20composite%3A%20Capacity%20(40%2C%20from%20live%20cores%20vs%20plan)%2C%20Trend%20(20)%2C%20Anomalies%20(20)%2C%20Memory%20(10%2C%20from%20live%20GB%20vs%20plan)%2C%20Concentration%2FHHI%20(10).%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22A%20quick-glance%20check%20during%20the%20day.%20Drop%20into%20the%20gauges%20%2B%20alerts%20panel%20below%20if%20you%20want%20detail.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A%22Health%20%E2%86%92%20top%20banner%22%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22barometer%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Load%20Barometer%20(last%2030%20min)%22%2Clayman%3A%22The%20most%20up-to-the-second%20view%20of%20how%20the%20server%20is%20doing.%20Refreshes%20whenever%20you%20reload%20the%20SiteGround%20stats%20panel%20at%2015-second%20granularity.%22%2Ctechnical%3A'Cores%20in%20use%20at%2015-second%20resolution%20from%20%3Ccode%3Estep%3Dm3%3C%2Fcode%3E%20(raw%20API%20value%20%2F%20100).%20Only%20the%20last%2030%20minutes%20is%20available%3B%20useful%20as%20an%20%22is%20it%20bad%20RIGHT%20NOW%3F%22%20probe.%2075%25-of-plan%20threshold%20line%20scales%20with%20plan%20size.'%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Investigating%20a%20live%20incident%20or%20user-reported%20slowness.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A'Health%20%E2%86%92%20%22CPU%20Core%20Barometer%22%20panel'%7D%5D%7D%2C%7Bsection%3A%22Caveats%20%26%20corrections%22%2Citems%3A%5B%7Bkey%3A%22plan_change%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Plan%20Change%22%2Clayman%3A%22SiteGround%20changed%20your%20account's%20core%2FRAM%20allocation%20mid-period.%20The%20dashboard%20handles%20this%20automatically%20%E2%80%94%20every%20metric%20is%20in%20absolute%20units%20(cores%20in%20use%2C%20GB%20used%2C%20CPU%20seconds)%2C%20so%20the%20same%20workload%20appears%20as%20the%20same%20number%20regardless%20of%20which%20plan%20was%20active.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Detected%20from%20%3Ccode%3Elimits_list%3C%2Fcode%3E%20step-changes.%20Marked%20with%20a%20vertical%20purple%20line%20on%20multi-day%20charts.%20%3Cstrong%3EEverything%20on%20this%20dashboard%20is%20plan-immune%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20CPU%20seconds%2C%20program%20executions%2C%20cores%20in%20use%20(raw%20API%20value%20%2F%20100)%2C%20and%20memory%20GB%20are%20all%20absolute%20counts%20that%20don't%20change%20when%20the%20plan%20does.%20Only%20the%20%5C%22%25%20of%20plan%5C%22%20subtitle%20next%20to%20each%20absolute%20reading%20rescales%20%E2%80%94%20a%20day%20that%20used%201.47%20cores%20was%2021%25%20of%20a%207-core%20plan%2C%20now%20it's%2016.3%25%20of%20a%209-core%20plan.%20The%20cores%20number%20is%20unchanged.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Never%20a%20blocker.%20Every%20chart%20and%20table%20is%20comparable%20across%20the%20plan-change%20date.%20The%20purple%20marker%20is%20purely%20informational.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A%22Trends%20%E2%86%92%20top%20banner%20%C2%B7%20Before%2FAfter%20%E2%86%92%20credibility%20card%20%C2%B7%20Every%20multi-day%20chart%20marks%20the%20date%22%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22incomplete_day%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Partial%20Day%22%2Clayman%3A%22Today%20isn't%20over%20yet%2C%20so%20we%20don't%20count%20it%20in%20averages.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22Flagged%20when%20today's%20account%20CPU%20is%20%26lt%3B25%25%20of%20the%207-day%20rolling%20average.%20Excluded%20from%20baselines%2C%20benchmarks%2C%20and%20Before%2FAfter%20windows.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Stops%20today's%20in-progress%20total%20from%20dragging%20down%20comparisons.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A%22Anywhere%20a%20partial-day%20badge%20appears%22%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22tz_note%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Timezone%20Note%22%2Clayman%3A'All%20%22day%22%20boundaries%20on%20this%20dashboard%20are%20UTC.%20If%20you%5C're%20in%20Ireland%2C%20late-night%20activity%20around%2023%3A00%E2%80%9300%3A00%20may%20land%20on%20the%20next%20day%20in%20this%20view.'%2Ctechnical%3A%22SG's%20API%20serves%20UTC%20timestamps%20and%20we%20currently%20format%20dates%20with%20%3Ccode%3EtoISOString().slice(0%2C10)%3C%2Fcode%3E.%20Day-of-week%20pairing%20also%20uses%20%3Ccode%3EgetUTCDay()%3C%2Fcode%3E.%20No%20timezone%20toggle%20yet.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A%22Investigating%20very%20specific%20time-of-day%20events%20near%20midnight%20Dublin%20time.%20Otherwise%20negligible.%22%2CwhereToFind%3A%22Everywhere%22%7D%2C%7Bkey%3A%22blended_30d%22%2Ctitle%3A%2230d%20Averages%20Across%20a%20Plan%20Change%22%2Clayman%3A%22No%20special%20handling%20needed%20%E2%80%94%20every%20metric%20on%20this%20dashboard%20is%20in%20absolute%20units%20(cores%20used%2C%20GB%2C%20CPU%20seconds)%2C%20so%2030-day%20averages%20stay%20comparable%20across%20a%20plan%20upgrade%20automatically.%22%2Ctechnical%3A%22The%2030d%20benchmarks%20read%20from%20%3Ccode%3EcoresUsedMap%3C%2Fcode%3E%20(cores%20in%20use)%20and%20%3Ccode%3EmemDailyGbMap%3C%2Fcode%3E%20(GB)%20%E2%80%94%20both%20plan-immune%20by%20construction.%20No%20re-expression%20needed.%20Trajectory%20regression%20also%20runs%20on%20cores%3B%20slope%20unit%20is%20cores%2Fday.%20%3Ccode%3EcoreNormMap%3C%2Fcode%3E%20(%25%20of%20today's%20plan)%20and%20%3Ccode%3EmemDailyNormMap%3C%2Fcode%3E%20are%20retained%20for%20the%20rare%20capacity-planning%20subtitle%20only.%22%2CwhenItMatters%3A'Mostly%20informational.%20The%20%22of%20N%20cores%22%20or%20%22of%20N%20GB%22%20subtitle%20on%20every%20benchmark%20stays%20useful%20even%20though%20the%20underlying%20number%20is%20already%20plan-immune.'%2CwhereToFind%3A'Trends%20%E2%86%92%20bench-strip%20%E2%86%92%20%22Cores%20in%20Use%22%20%2F%20%22Memory%20(GB)%22%20items'%7D%5D%7D%5D%2Cn%3D(()%3D%3E%7Bconst%20e%3D%7B%7D%3Breturn%20a.forEach(t%3D%3Et.items.forEach(t%3D%3Ee%5Bt.key%5D%3Dt.layman))%2Ce%7D)()%2Cs%3De%3D%3En%5Be%5D%3F%60%3Cdiv%20class%3D%22layman-sub%22%3E%24%7Bn%5Be%5D%7D%3C%2Fdiv%3E%60%3A%22%22%2Co%3D%7BechartsUrl%3 rong%3ECPU%20vs%20Memory%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20CPU%20spikes%20mean%20slow%20page%20loads.%20Memory%20near%2085%25%20means%20risk%20of%20out-of-memory%20crashes.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3ETrajectory%20gauge%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20if%20%22%E2%86%92%2075%25%20in%204d%22%20is%20showing%2C%20plan%20to%20investigate%20before%20the%20weekend.%3C%2Fli%3E'%7D%2Csites%3A%7Bico%3A%22%F0%9F%8C%90%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Per-site%20usage%20and%20efficiency%22%2Cbody%3A'Compare%20your%20sites%20side-by-side.%20The%20%3Cstrong%3ECPU%20%2F%20Execution%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20bar%20chart%20is%20the%20most%20important%20%E2%80%94%20it%20shows%20the%20%3Cem%3Ecost%20of%20each%20request%3C%2Fem%3E%2C%20separating%20%22lots%20of%20traffic%22%20from%20%22each%20request%20is%20heavy%22.%20Sortable%20table%20at%20the%20bottom%20has%20everything.'%2Cmore%3A%22%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3ETotal%20CPU%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20total%20work%20the%20site%20did%20over%2030%20days.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3EAvg%2FDay%20%26%207d%20Avg%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20daily%20averages%20%E2%80%94%20use%20the%207d%20to%20see%20recent%20behaviour.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3EPeak%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20the%20worst%20single%20day.%20If%20peak%20is%20%26gt%3B10%C3%97%20average%2C%20the%20site%20is%20spiky%20and%20unstable.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3E%25%20Acct%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20what%20fraction%20of%20your%20whole%20account%20this%20site%20uses.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3ECPU%2FExec%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20the%20cost%20per%20PHP%20request.%20Below%201%20sec%2Fexec%20is%20great.%20Above%203%20sec%2Fexec%20means%20each%20request%20is%20expensive%20%E2%80%94%20usually%20plugins%2C%20queries%2C%20or%20SCORM%20saves.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3E7d%20Trend%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20positive%20means%20CPU%20is%20climbing%20week-on-week%2C%20negative%20means%20improving.%3C%2Fli%3E%22%7D%2Ccompare%3A%7Bico%3A%22%F0%9F%94%AC%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Did%20your%20fix%20work%3F%20Prove%20it.%22%2Cbody%3A%22Pick%20a%20site%2C%20a%20fix%20date%2C%20and%20how%20many%20days%20to%20compare%20before%2Fafter.%20The%20dashboard%20uses%20a%20%3Cstrong%3Econtrol%20group%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20of%20other%20sites%20to%20rule%20out%20ambient%20traffic%20changes%20%E2%80%94%20if%20everyone%20got%20quieter%2C%20that's%20not%20your%20fix.%20The%20interpretation%20paragraph%20translates%20the%20numbers%20into%20a%20story%20you%20can%20show%20stakeholders.%22%2Cmore%3A%22%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3ETarget%20Share%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20the%20target's%20%25%20of%20total%20account%20CPU.%20If%20this%20drops%2C%20your%20site%20became%20proportionally%20less%20dominant%20%E2%80%94%20regardless%20of%20overall%20traffic.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3ETarget%20%2F%20Control%20ratio%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20the%20gold-standard%20metric.%20If%20it%20falls%20significantly%2C%20your%20fix%20is%20the%20cause%2C%20not%20background%20noise.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3EWelch's%20t-test%20p-value%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20statistical%20significance.%20p%20%26lt%3B%200.05%20means%20the%20change%20is%20unlikely%20to%20be%20coincidence.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3E5%E2%80%937%20days%20of%20%5C%22after%5C%22%20data%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20is%20recommended%20before%20declaring%20success.%20Earlier%20than%20that%20and%20one%20busy%20day%20can%20skew%20everything.%3C%2Fli%3E%22%7D%2Ctrends%3A%7Bico%3A%22%F0%9F%93%88%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Patterns%20over%20time%22%2Cbody%3A%22Switch%20between%20Day%20%2F%20Hour%20%2F%203-Min%20resolution%20to%20zoom%20into%20different%20timeframes.%20The%20%3Cstrong%3Eday-of-week%20pattern%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20reveals%20recurring%20traffic%20patterns%20(Monday%20spikes%2C%20weekend%20lulls).%20The%20%3Cstrong%3Etrajectory%20line%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20projects%20where%20you're%20heading%20if%20current%20trends%20continue.%22%2Cmore%3A%22%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3EDaily%20view%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%2030-day%20picture.%20Look%20for%20week-on-week%20climb%20(bad)%20or%20step-changes%20after%20a%20fix%20(good).%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3EHourly%20view%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20last%2024h.%20Shows%20the%20day's%20rhythm%20and%20which%20hours%20were%20busy.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3E3-Min%20view%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20live%20pressure%20at%2015-second%20resolution.%20Use%20this%20when%20you%20think%20the%20server%20is%20currently%20struggling.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3EDay-of-Week%20pattern%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20shows%20the%20average%20%2B%20peak%20CPU%20per%20weekday.%20Bars%20with%20much%20higher%20peak%20than%20average%20mean%20that%20day%20is%20unstable.%3C%2Fli%3E%22%7D%2Craw%3A%7Bico%3A%22%F0%9F%93%8B%22%2Ctitle%3A%22Source%20data%20%E2%80%94%20every%20number%2C%20every%20day%22%2Cbody%3A%22A%20flat%20table%20of%20every%20daily%20metric%20per%20site.%20Use%20the%20%3Cstrong%3EExport%20CSV%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20button%20to%20pull%20this%20into%20Excel%20or%20Google%20Sheets%20for%20deeper%20analysis.%20The%20Group%20dropdown%20changes%20which%20sites%20are%20shown%20as%20columns.%22%2Cmore%3A%22%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3EAcct%20CPU%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20total%20account-wide%20CPU%20for%20the%20day.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3ECores%20Used%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20cores%20in%20use%20for%20the%20day%20(out%20of%20plan%20total).%20Red%20when%20over%2075%25%20of%20plan.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3EMemory%20(GB)%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20day's%20peak%20memory%20used.%20Red%20over%2085%25%20of%20plan.%3C%2Fli%3E%3Cli%3E%3Cstrong%3Epartial%20badge%3A%3C%2Fstrong%3E%20today's%20data%20is%20still%20accumulating%20%E2%80%94%20excluded%20from%20averages%20and%20trends.%3C%2Fli%3E%22%7D%7D%5Be%5D%3Breturn%20t%3F%60%3Cdiv%20class%3D%22intro-card%22%3E%3Cdiv%20class%3D%22ico%22%3E%24%7Bt.ico%7D%3C%2Fdiv%3E%3Cdiv%20class%3D%22body%22%3E%3Ch4%3E%24%7Bz(t.title)%7D%3C%2Fh4%3E%3Cdiv%3E%24%7Bt.body%7D%3C%2Fdiv%3E%3Cdetails%20open%3E%3Csummary%3EHow%20to%20read%20this%20in%20detail%3C%2Fsummary%3E%3Cdiv%20class%3D%22more%22%3E%3Cul%3E%24%7Bt.more%7D%3C%2Ful%3E%3C%2Fdiv%3E%3C%2Fdetails%3E%3C%2Fdiv%3E%3C%2Fdiv%3E%60%3A%22%22%7D%2Cxe%3De%3D%3E%7Bconst%20t%3Ddocument.getElementById(%22sgd-body%2 %20%20%20%20%E2%80%A2%20cyan%20%2327AAE1%20as%20the%20signature%20accent%20%E2%80%94%20used%20as%203px%20top%20borders%20on%20hero%20cards%2C%5Cn%20%20%20%20%20%20%203px%20left%20borders%20on%20active%20table%20rows%2C%20and%20bottom-border%20separators%5Cn%20%20%20%20%20%E2%80%A2%20soft%20shadows%20for%20surface%20lift%2C%20no%20glow%5Cn%20%20%20DARK%20is%20independently%20designed%20for%20after-hours%20analyst%20work%2C%20NOT%20a%20lazy%20inversion%3A%5Cn%20%20%20%20%20%E2%80%A2%20deep%20blue-grey%20ground%20(%230F1419)%2C%20cards%20lifted%20with%201px%20white-alpha%20border%5Cn%20%20%20%20%20%E2%80%A2%20cyan-glow%20accents%20(lighter%20borders%20%2B%20subtle%20outer%20glow)%20instead%20of%20top%20bars%5Cn%20%20%20%20%20%E2%80%A2%20saturated%20accent%20colours%20to%20read%20clearly%20against%20dark%20surfaces%5Cn%20%20%20Both%20share%20Montserrat%20typography%20and%20the%20same%20accent%20palette.%20Everything%20else%20differs.%20*%2F%5Cn%5Cn%2F*%20%E2%94%80%E2%94%80%20LIGHT%20(default)%20%E2%94%80%E2%94%80%20*%2F%5Cn%23sgd%2C%20.sgd-wait%2C%20%23sgd-tt%2C%20.drill-modal%20%7B%5Cn%20%20--bg%3A%20%23F8F9FB%3B%20--bg-card%3A%20%23FFFFFF%3B%20--bg-card-alt%3A%20%23F8F9FB%3B%5Cn%20%20--bg-input%3A%20%23FFFFFF%3B%20--bg-header%3A%20linear-gradient(135deg%2C%23FFFFFF%2C%23F0F8FC)%3B%5Cn%20%20--bg-tab%3A%20%23FFFFFF%3B%5Cn%20%20--text%3A%20%233D3D3D%3B%20--text-strong%3A%20%231A1A1A%3B%20--text-muted%3A%20%23555E68%3B%5Cn%20%20--text-dim%3A%20%236e7785%3B%20--text-faint%3A%20%23939393%3B%20--text-ghost%3A%20%23B5BCC4%3B%5Cn%20%20--text-meta%3A%20%23D8DEE5%3B%5Cn%20%20--border%3A%20%23E8ECF0%3B%20--border-strong%3A%20%23C5CDD6%3B%5Cn%20%20--border-faint%3A%20%23F0F2F5%3B%5Cn%20%20--hover-row%3A%20rgba(39%2C170%2C225%2C0.035)%3B%5Cn%20%20--row-hi%3A%20rgba(39%2C170%2C225%2C0.07)%3B%5Cn%20%20--shadow%3A%200%204px%2018px%20rgba(61%2C61%2C61%2C0.06)%3B%5Cn%20%20--shadow-card%3A%200%201px%202px%20rgba(61%2C61%2C61%2C0.04)%3B%5Cn%20%20--shadow-hero%3A%200%204px%2014px%20rgba(39 %20The%20header%20content%20sits%20in%20a%20centered%20max-width%20column%20so%20the%20logo%2Ftitle%20align%20vertically%5Cn%20%20%20with%20the%20content%20cards%20in%20the%20body.%20The%20tab%20nav%20has%20the%20same%20outer%20padding%20but%20its%20inner%5Cn%20%20%20flow%20is%20full-width%20%E2%80%94%20tabs%20spread%20naturally%20edge-to-edge%20with%20the%20active%20underline%20reading%5Cn%20%20%20correctly%20across%20the%20page.%20*%2F%5Cn.sgd-hdr%2C.sgd-tabs%7Bpadding-left%3Avar(--pad-body-h)%3Bpadding-right%3Avar(--pad-body-h)%7D%5Cn.sgd-hdr-inner%7Bmax-width%3Avar(--max-content-w)%3Bmargin%3A0%20auto%3Bwidth%3A100%25%3Bdisplay%3Aflex%3Balign-items%3Acenter%3Bgap%3A10px%3Bflex-wrap%3Awrap%7D%5Cn%2F*%20Header-specific%20compaction%3A%20zero%20out%20every%20descendant's%20padding%2Fmargin%20so%20the%20strip%20stays%5Cn%20%20%20on%20one%20line%2C%20then%20opt-in%20to%20padding%20ONLY%20for%20the%20buttons%20that%20need%20it.%20This%20is%20the%20right%5Cn%20%20%20place%20for%20a%20wildcard%20reset%20%E2%80%94%20the%20header%20is%20a%20tight%20horizontal%20strip%20where%20defaults%20from%5Cn%20%20%20component%20classes%20are%20too%20generous.%20*%2F%5Cn.sgd-hdr-inner%20*%7Bpadding%3A0%3Bmargin%3A0%7D%5Cn.sgd-hdr-inner%20.sgd-hdr-acts%7Bmargin-left%3Aauto%3Bdisplay%3Aflex%3Balign-items%3Acenter%3Bgap%3A6px%7D%5Cn.sgd-hdr-inner%20.btn.sm%7Bpadding%3A6px%2011px%3Bfont-size%3A11.5px%3Bborder-radius%3A7px%7D%5Cn.sgd-hdr-inner%20.theme-toggle%7Bwidth%3A28px%3Bheight%3A28px%3Bborder-radius%3A7px%3Bfont-size%3A13px%3Bdisplay%3Ainline-flex%3Balign-items%3Acenter%3Bjustify-content%3Acenter%7D%5Cn.sgd-hdr-inner%20.excl-chip%7Bpadding%3A5px%2011px%3Bfont-size%3A11.5px%3Bdisplay%3Ainline-flex%3Balign-items%3Acenter%3Bgap%3A5px%3Bborder-radius%3Avar(--radius-pill)%7D%5Cn.sgd-hdr-inner%20.av-b%7Bpadding%3A3px%208px%3Bfont-size%3A9.5px%3Bdisplay%3Ainline-flex%3Balign-items%3Acenter%3Bgap%3A4px%3Bborder-radius%3Avar(--radius-pill)%7D%5Cn.sgd-hdr-inner%20h1%7Bfont-size%3A14.5px%3Bline ---

### `ie-extractor.js`

This script creates a floating image extractor panel on any page, which collects all images, detects if they are from iStock, and displays them in a sortable table. You can filter images by filename, alt text, or iStock ID, select images, and download their files directly. It enhances image management without altering page content permanently.

**Usage**

- Run the bookmarklet while on a page with images to activate the extractor panel.
- Click the 🖼 Images FAB button to show/hide the panel.
- Use the search box or filters to find specific images by filename, alt, or iStock ID.
- Click download options in the panel to save selected images.

**jsDelivr URL**

```
https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/CobblestoneLearning/Chrome-Scripts@main/ie-extractor.js
```

**Bookmarklet** — create a new bookmark and paste this as the URL, or drag the snippet into your bookmarks bar:

